    WHEN [v].[VehicleId] IS NOT NULL THEN [v].[RegistrationNumber]
    ELSE N'Not Assigned'
END AS [Vehicle], CASE
    WHEN [u].[UserId] IS NOT NULL THEN [u].[FullName]
    ELSE N'Not Assigned'
END AS [Driver]
FROM [Trip] AS [t]
INNER JOIN [Routes] AS [r] ON [t].[RouteId] = [r].[RouteId]
LEFT JOIN [Vehicles] AS [v] ON [t].[VehicleId] = [v].[VehicleId]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[DriverId] = [u].[UserId]
